L // The latency benchmark attempts to model the speed of the hash function in
T 2 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp punctuation.definition.comment.cpp
T 76 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp
L // production. When a hash function is used for hashtable lookups it is rarely
T 2 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp punctuation.definition.comment.cpp
T 78 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp
L // used to hash N items in a tight loop nor on constant sized strings. Instead,
T 2 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp punctuation.definition.comment.cpp
T 79 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp
L // after hashing there is a potential equality test plus a (usually) large
T 2 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp punctuation.definition.comment.cpp
T 74 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp
L // amount of user code. To simulate this effectively we introduce a data
T 2 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp punctuation.definition.comment.cpp
T 72 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp
L // dependency between elements we hash by using the hash of the Nth element as
T 2 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp punctuation.definition.comment.cpp
T 78 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp
L // the selector of the N+1th element to hash. This isolates the hash function
T 2 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp punctuation.definition.comment.cpp
T 77 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp
L // code much like in production. As a bonus we use the hash to generate strings
T 2 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp punctuation.definition.comment.cpp
T 79 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp
L // of size [1,N] (instead of fixed N) to disable perfect branch predictions in
T 2 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp punctuation.definition.comment.cpp
T 78 source.cpp comment.line.double-slash.cpp
L // hash function implementations.
